      INNER JOIN [EXERCICE_BUDGETAIRE] AS [e] ON [v].[FK_ExerciceBudgetaire] = [e].[IdExercice]
      LEFT JOIN [VERSION_BUDGETAIRE] AS [v0] ON [v].[FK_VersionBudgetairePrecedente] = [v0].[IdVersion]
